Company and its subsidiaries (the “Group”, “we”, “our” or “us”)           二五年十二月三十一日止年度（「二零二五年」、
for the year ended 31 December 2025 (the “2025”, “Year”, “FY              「本 年 度」、「二 零 二 五 財 政 年 度」或「報 告 期 內」

value.” By deepening service excellence and advancing business             信任根基，以前瞻性的業態佈局搶佔增長先機，

buildings, villas and tourist towns.

                                                                                                     主席報告


On the business model innovation front, the Group vigorously              在業態創新方面，本集團大力佈局「物業 +」戰略，
advanced the “Property+” strategy, with a particular focus on             重點推進文旅康養業務落地，搶佔新興產業賽道

                                                                          Corporate Governance Report
                                                                                                          企業管治報告


The Board is pleased to report to the shareholders of the Company (the        董事會欣然向本公司股東（「股東」）報告本公司於
“Shareholder(s)“) on the corporate governance of the Company                  年內的企業管治情況。
during the Year.


CORPORATE GOVERNANCE PRACTICES                                                企業管治常規

The Board is committed to achieving high corporate governance                 董事會致力實現高水平企業管治標準。
standards.

The Board believes that high corporate governance standards and a             董事會相信，高水平企業管治標準及開放文化對
culture of openness are essential in providing a framework for the            於為本集團提供保障股東利 益，提升企業價值、
Group to safeguard the interests of Shareholders, enhance corporate           制定其業務戰略及政策、促進有效貢獻以及加強
value, formulate its business strategies and policies, facilitate effective   其透明度及問責性的框架至關重要，以使股東評
contribution, and enhance its transparency and accountability, thereby        估本公司應用聯交所證券上市規則（「上市規則」）
enabling Shareholders’ evaluation of the Company’s application of             附錄 C1「第二部分－良好企業管治的原則、守則
the principles and code provisions of the Corporate Governance Code           條文及建議最佳常規」所載企業管治守則（ 「企業
(the “CG Code”) contained in “Part 2 – Principles of Good Corporate           管治守則」）之原則及守則條文之情況。
Governance, Code Provisions and Recommended Best Practices” of
Appendix C1 to the Rules Governing the Listing of Securities on the
Stock Exchange (the “Listing Rules“).

the Company’s website.

3.    BASIS OF PREPARATION AND MATERIAL                                   3.   編製基準及重大會計政策
      ACCOUNTING POLICIES

      The consolidated financial statements have been prepared on              綜合財務報表已按歷史成本基準（惟按公
      the historical cost basis, except for equity instrument at FVTPL         平值計入損益的股本工具及按公平值計入
      and equity instruments at FVTOCI that are measured at fair               其他全面收益的股本工具乃按公平值計量
      values, and in accordance with IFRS Accounting Standards                 除外）及按照國際會計準則理事會頒佈的
      issued by the IASB. For the purpose of preparation of the                國際財務報告準則會計準則編製。就編製
      consolidated financial statements, information is considered             綜合財務報表而言，倘有關資料合理預期
      material if such information is reasonably expected to influence         會影響主要用戶作出之決定，則有關資料
      decisions made by primary users. In addition, the consolidated           被視為重大。此外，綜合財務報表包括聯
      financial statements include applicable disclosures required             交所證券上市規則（「上市規則」）及香港公
      by the Rules Governing the Listing of Securities on the Stock            司條例規定的適用披露事項。
      Exchange (the “Listing Rules”) and by the Hong Kong
      Companies Ordinance.

      Historical cost is generally based on the fair value of the              歷史成本一般根據換取商品及服務所付代
      consideration given in exchange for goods and services.                  價的公平值計算。

      Fair value is the price that would be received to sell an asset          公平值是指市場參與者之間在計量日進行
      or paid to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction between        的有序交易中出售一項資產所收取的價格
      market participants at the measurement date, regardless of               或轉移一項負債所支付的價格，無論該價
      whether that price is directly observable or estimated using             格是直接觀察到的結果還是採用其他估值
      another valuation technique. In estimating the fair value                技術作出的估計。在對資產或負債的公平
      of an asset or a liability, the Group takes into account the             值作出估計時，本集團考慮了市場參與者
      characteristics of the asset or liability if market participants         在計量日為該資產或負債進行定價時將會
      would take those characteristics into account when pricing               考慮的該等特徵。在本綜合財務報表中計
      the asset or liability at the measurement date. Fair value for           量及╱或披露的公平值均在此基礎上予以
      measurement and/or disclosure purposes in these consolidated             確定，但國際財務報告準則第 2 號以股份
      financial statements is determined on such a basis, except for           為基礎的支付 範圍內的以股份為基礎的支
      share-based payment transactions that are within the scope               付 交 易、根 據 國 際 財 務 報 告 準 則 第 16 號
      of IFRS 2 Share-based Payment , leasing transactions that                租賃 入賬的租賃交易、以及與公平值類似
      are accounted for in accordance with IFRS 16 Leases , and                但並非公平值的計量（例如，國際會計準
      measurements that have some similarities to fair value but are           則第 2 號存貨 的可變現淨值或國際會計準
      not fair value, such as net realisable value in IAS 2 Inventories        則第 36 號資產減值 中的使用價值）除外。
      or value in use in IAS 36 Impairment of Assets .

      A fair value measurement of a non-financial asset takes into             非金融資產之公平值計量考慮了市場參與
      account a market participant’s ability to generate economic              者可透過按該資產的最高及最佳用途使用
      benefits by using the asset in its highest and best use or by            該資產，或將該資產售予另一可按該資產
      selling it to another market participant that would use the              的最高及最佳用途使用該資產的市場參與
      asset in its highest and best use.                                       者，從而產生經濟利益的能力。

3.   BASIS OF PREPARATION AND MATERIAL                                    3.   編製基準及重大會計政策（續）
     ACCOUNTING POLICIES (Continued)

     For financial instruments which is transacted at fair value and           就按公平值進行交易之金融工具以及於隨
     a valuation technique that unobservable inputs are to be used             後期間將使用不可觀察輸入數據計量公平
     to measure fair value in subsequent periods, the valuation                值的估值方法而言，估值方法會予以校準，
     technique is calibrated so that at initial recognition the results        以使於初步確認時估值方法的結果與交易
     of the valuation technique equals the transaction price.                  價格相等。

     In addition, for financial reporting purposes, fair value                 此外，就財務報告而言，公平值計量根據
     measurements are categorised into Level 1, 2 or 3 based on                公平值計量的輸入數據可觀察程度及公平
     the degree to which the inputs to the fair value measurements             值計量的輸入數據對其整體的重要性分類
     are observable and the significance of the inputs to the fair             為第一級、第二級或第三級，詳情如下：
     value measurement in its entirety, which are described as
     follows:

     •      Level 1 inputs are quoted prices (unadjusted) in active            •        第一級輸入數據乃本集團於計量日
            markets for identical assets or liabilities that the Group                  可取得的相同資產或負債於活躍市
            can access at the measurement date;                                         場之報價（未經調整）；

     •      Level 2 inputs are inputs, other than quoted prices                •        第二級輸入數據乃就資產或負債直
            included within Level 1, that are observable for the                        接或間接地可觀察之輸入數據（第
            asset or liability, either directly or indirectly; and                      一級內包括的報價除外）；及

     •      Level 3 inputs are unobservable inputs for the asset or            •        第三級輸入數據乃資產或負債的不
            liability.                                                                  可觀察輸入數據。

     The material accounting policies applied in the preparation of            編製該等綜合財務報表所應用的重大會計
     these consolidated financial statement are set out below.                 政策載列如下。

     Basis of consolidation                                                    綜合基準

     The consolidated financial statements incorporate the financial           綜合財務報表包括本公司及本公司的控制
     statements of the Company and entities controlled by the                  實體及附屬公司的財務報表。本公司在以
     Company and its subsidiaries. Control is achieved when the                下情況下取得控制權：
     Company:

     •      has power over the investee;                                       •        可對被投資方行使權力；

     •      is exposed, or has rights, to variable returns from its            •        就來自參與被投資方業務的可變回
            involvement with the investee; and                                          報承受風險或享有權利；及

     •      has the ability to use its power to affect its returns.            •        可行使權力以影響其回報。

     The Group reassesses whether or not it controls an investee if            倘事實及情況顯示上述控制權三個因素中
     facts and circumstances indicate that there are changes to one            的一個或以上發生變化，本集團會重新評
     or more of the three elements of control listed above.                    估其是否擁有被投資方的控制權。

3.    BASIS OF PREPARATION AND MATERIAL                                   3.   編製基準及重大會計政策（續）
      ACCOUNTING POLICIES (Continued)

      Basis of consolidation (Continued)                                       綜合基準（續）

      Consolidation of a subsidiary begins when the Group obtains              當本集團取得附屬公司的控制權時，開始
      control over the subsidiary and ceases when the Group loses              對附屬公司綜合入賬，並於本集團失去對
      control of the subsidiary. Specifically, results of a subsidiary         該附屬公司的控制權時終止綜合入賬。具
      acquired or disposed of during the year are included in                  體而言，於年內所收購或出售的附屬公司
      the consolidated statement of profit or loss and other                   的業績由本集團取得控制權當日起直至本
      comprehensive income from the date of the Group gains                    集團不再對該附屬公司擁有控制權之日止
      control until the date when the Group ceases to control the              計入綜合損益及其他全面收益表。
      subsidiary.

      Profit or loss and each item of other comprehensive income               損益及其他全面收益各項目歸屬於本公司
      are attributed to the owners of the Company and to the                   擁有人及非控股權益。附屬公司的全面收
      non-controlling interests. Total comprehensive income of                 益總額歸於本公司擁有人及非控股權益，
      subsidiaries is attributed to the owners of the Company and              即使此舉會導致非控股權益產生虧絀結餘。
      to the non-controlling interests even if this results in the non-
      controlling interests having a deficit balance.

      Where necessary, adjustments are made to the financial                   如必要，附屬公司的財務報表會作出調整，
      statements of subsidiaries to bring their accounting policies in         以令其會計政策與本集團的會計政策一致。
      line with the Group’s accounting policies.

      All intra-group assets and liabilities, equity, income, expenses         本集團成員公司之間交易所產生的所有集
      and cash flows relating to transactions between members of               團內公司間資產、負債、權益、收入、開
      the Group are eliminated in full on consolidation.                       支及現金流量均在綜合入賬時全數對銷。

      Non-controlling interests in subsidiaries are presented                  附屬公司的非控股權益與本集團於當中的
      separately from the Group’s equity therein, which represent              權益分開呈列，指現時擁有權權益賦予持
      present ownership interests entitling their holders to a                 有人權利於清盤時按比例分佔相關附屬公
      proportionate share of net assets of the relevant subsidiaries           司資產淨值。
      upon liquidation.

      Investments in associates and joint ventures                            於聯營公司及合營企業的投資

      An associate is an entity over which the Group has significant          聯營公司為本集團對其具有重大影響力的
      influence. Significant influence is the power to participate in         實體。重大影響力指參與投資對象財務及
      the financial and operating policy decisions of the investee but        經營政策決策的權力，惟並非控制或共同
      is not control or joint control over those policies.                    控制該等政策。

      A joint venture is a joint arrangement whereby the parties              合營企業指一項合營安排，對安排擁有共
      that have joint control of the arrangement have rights to               同控制權之訂約方據此對合營安排之資產
      the net assets of the joint arrangement. Joint control is the           淨值擁有權利。共同控制是指按照合約約
      contractually agreed sharing of control of an arrangement,              定對某項安排所共有的控制，共同控制僅
      which exists only when decisions about the relevant activities          在當相關活動要求共同享有控制權之各方
      require unanimous consent of the parties sharing control.               作出一致同意之決定時存在。

      Revenue from contracts with customers                                     客戶合約收益

      The Group recognises revenue when (or as) a performance                   本集團於完成履約責任時（或就此）確認收
      obligation is satisfied, i.e. when “control” of the services              益，即於特定履約責任相關服務的「控制權」
      underlying the particular performance obligation is transferred           轉讓予客戶時確認收益。
      to customers.

      A performance obligation represents a good or service (or                 履約責任指一項明確貨品或服務（或一批
      a bundle of goods or services) that is distinct or a series of            貨品或服務）或一系列大致相同的明確貨
      distinct goods or services that are substantially the same.               品或服務。

     Control is transferred over time and revenue is recognised             倘符合以下其中一項條件，控制權會隨時
     over time by reference to the progress towards complete                間轉移，而收益則參考相關履約責任的完
     satisfaction of the relevant performance obligation if one of          成進度隨時間確認：
     the following criteria is met:

     •      the customer simultaneously receives and consumes               •        客戶在本集團履約之同時取得及消
            the benefits provided by the Group’s performance as                      費其履約所提供之利益；
            the Group performs;

     •      the Group’s performance creates or enhances an asset            •        於本集團履約時，其履約創造或增
            that the customer controls as the Group performs; or                     強由客戶控制之資產；或

     •      the Group’s performance does not create an asset                •        本集團之履約並無創造一項可被其
            with an alternative use to the Group and the Group                       用於替代用途之資產，並且本集團
            has an enforceable right to payment for performance                      具有就迄今為止已完成之履約部分
            completed to date.                                                       獲得客戶付款之可執行權利。

     Otherwise, revenue is recognised at a point in time when the           否則，收益於客戶取得明確貨品或服務控
     customer obtains control of the distinct good or service.              制權時確認。

     Contract assets and contract liabilities                               合約資產及合約負債

     A contract asset represents the Group’s right to consideration         合約資產指本集團就換取本集團已向客戶
     in exchange for goods or services that the Group has                   轉讓的商品或服務而收取代價的權利（尚
     transferred to a customer that is not yet unconditional. It is         未成為無條件），根據國際財務報告準則
     assessed for impairment in accordance with IFRS 9. In contrast,        第 9 號評估減值。相反，應收款項指本集
     a receivable represents the Group’s unconditional right to             團收取代價的無條件權利，即代價到期付
     consideration, i.e. only the passage of time is required before        款前僅需時間推移。合約負債指本集團因
     payment of that consideration is due. A contract liability             已向客戶收取代價（或已到期代價），而須
     represents the Group’s obligation to transfer goods or services        向客戶轉讓商品或服務的責任。
     to a customer for which the Group has received consideration
     (or an amount of consideration is due) from the customer.

     A contract asset and a contract liability relating to the same         與相同合約有關的合約資產及合約負債按
     contract are accounted for and presented on a net basis.               淨額基準入賬及呈列。

      Existence of significant financing component                             存在重大融資成份

      In determining the transaction price, the Group adjusts the              於釐定交易價時，倘向客戶轉移貨品或服
      promised amount of consideration for the effects of the                  務 時（不 論 以 明 示 或 暗 示 方 式）協 定 之 付
      time value of money if the timing of payments agreed (either             款時間為客戶或本集團帶來重大融資利益，
      explicitly or implicitly) provides the customer or the Group             則本集團就金額時間值的影響而調整已承
      with a significant benefit of financing the transfer of goods or         諾之代價金額。於該等情況下，合約含有
      services to the customer. In those circumstances, the contract           重大融資成份。不論於合約中以明示呈列
      contains a significant financing component. A significant                或合約訂約方協定的支付條款暗示融資承
      financing component may exist regardless of whether the                  諾，合約中均存在重大融資成份。
      promise of financing is explicitly stated in the contract or
      implied by the payment terms agreed to by the parties to the
      contract.

      For contracts where the period between payments and transfer             就相關貨品或服務的支付與轉移期間少於
      of the associated goods or services is less than one year, the           一年的合約而言，本集團就任何重大融資
      Group applies the practical expedient of not adjusting the               成份應用不調整交易價的簡易處理方法。
      transaction price for any significant financing component.

      Principal versus agent                                                   委託人與代理

      When another party is involved in providing goods or services             當有另一方涉及向客戶提供貨品或服務時，
      to a customer, the Group determines whether the nature of its             本集團會釐定其承諾性質屬由其自身提供
      promise is a performance obligation to provide the specified              指 定 貨 品 或 服 務（即 本 集 團 為 委 託 人）或
      goods or services itself (i.e. the Group is a principal) or to            安排將由其他方提供該等貨品或服務
      arrange for those goods or services to be provided by the                （即本集團為代理）之履約責任。
      other party (i.e. the Group is an agent).

      The Group is a principal if it controls the specified good or            倘本集團於指定貨品或服務轉交客戶前控
      service before that good or service is transferred to a customer.        制該貨品或服務，則本集團為委託人。

      The Group is an agent if its performance obligation is to                倘本集團之履約責任為安排另一方提供指
      arrange for the provision of the specified good or service by            定貨品或服務，則本集團為代理。在此情
      another party. In this case, the Group does not control the              況下，本集團於另一方所提供指定貨品或
      specified good or service provided by another party before               服務轉交客戶前並無控制該貨品或服務。
      that good or service is transferred to the customer. When the            倘本集團以代理身份行事，將就其預期於
      Group acts as an agent, it recognises revenue in the amount              安排其他方提供指定貨品或服務時有權收
      of any fee or commission to which it expects to be entitled in           取之任何費用或佣金金額確認收入。
      exchange for arranging for the specified goods or services to
      be provided by the other party.

     Costs to fulfil a contract                                               履行合同的成本

     The Group incurs costs (including labour costs, utility expenses,        本集團於商業運營服務合約中產生履行合
     materials and consumables and others) to fulfil a contract in            約 的 成 本（包 括 勞 工 成 本、公 用 開 支、材
     its commercial operational services contracts. The Group first           料及消耗品以及其他）。本集團首先根據
     assesses whether these costs qualify for recognition as an asset         其他相關準則評估該等成本是否合資格確
     in terms of other relevant standards, failing which it recognises        認為資產，倘不合資格，僅在符合以下標
     an asset for these costs only if they meet all of the following          準後將該等成本確認為資產：
     criteria:

     •      the costs relate directly to a contract or to an                  •        有關成本與本集團可明確識別的合
            anticipated contract that the Group can specifically                       同或預期合同有直接關係；
            identify;

     •      the costs generate or enhance resources of the Group              •        有關成本令本集團將用於履行（或
            that will be used in satisfying (or in continuing to                       持續履行）日後履約義務之資源得
            satisfy) performance obligations in the future; and                        以產生或有所增加；及

     •      the costs are expected to be recovered.                           •        有關成本預期可收回。

     The asset so recognised is subsequently amortised to profit or           由此確認的資產隨後按系統性基準於損益
     loss on a systematic basis that is consistent with the transfer          攤銷，與轉讓予客戶商品或服務（與資產
     to the customer of the goods or services to which the assets             相關）相符。資產須進行減值審閱。
     relate. The asset is subject to impairment review.

     Deferred contract costs capitalised at the end of the reporting          於報告期末，資本化的遞延合約成本為合
     period are costs incurred to fulfill a contract, of which,               約履行成本，其中，履約責任尚未完成。
     the performance obligation is not yet satisfied. Deferred                遞延合約成本於履約責任獲達成及合約收
     contract costs are recognised as part of cost of services                益獲確認期間在綜合損益表內確認為服務
     in the consolidated statement of profit or loss and other                成本的一部分。
     comprehensive income in the period in which the performance
     obligation is satisfied and revenue related to the contract is
     recognised.

      Lease                                                                  租賃

      Definition of a lease                                                  租賃的定義

      A contract is, or contains, a lease if the contract conveys the        租賃是指一段時間內出租人將已識別資產
      right to control the use of an identified asset for a period of        的使用權移轉予承租人以獲得代價的合約。
      time in exchange for consideration.

      For contracts entered into or modified on or after the date of         就於首次應用日期或之後訂立、修訂或來
      initial application or arising from business combinations, the         自業務合併的合約而言，本集團於合約初
      Group assesses whether a contract is or contains a lease based         始日期、修訂日期或收購日期（如適用）根
      on the definition under IFRS 16 at inception, modification date        據 國 際 財 務 報 告 準 則 第 16 號 項 下 的 定 義
      or acquisition date, as appropriate. Such contract will not be         評估該合約是否為租賃或包括租賃。除非
      reassessed unless the terms and conditions of the contract             合約條款及條件發生變化，本集團不重新
      are subsequently changed. As a practical expedient, leases             評估合約是否為租賃或包含租賃。作為切
      with similar characteristics are accounted on a portfolio basis        實可行的權宜之計，當本集團合理預期對
      when the Group reasonably expects that the effects on the              綜合財務報表的影響與組合內單一租賃的
      consolidated financial statements would not differ materially          區別不大時，具有類似特徵的租賃按組合
      from individual leases within the portfolio.                           基準入賬。

      The Group as a lessee                                                  本集團作為承租人

      Allocation of consideration to components of a contract                分配代價至合約組成部分

      For a contract that contains a lease component and one or              就包含租賃組成部分及一項或多項額外租
      more additional lease or non-lease components, the Group               賃或非租賃組成部分的合約而言，本集團
      allocates the consideration in the contract to each lease              根據租賃組成部分的相對獨立價格及非租
      component on the basis of the relative stand-alone price of            賃組成部分的獨立價格總額將合約代價分
      the lease component and the aggregate stand-alone price of             配至各租賃組成部分。
      the non-lease components.

      The Group also applies practical expedient not to separate             本集團亦應用切實可行的權宜之計不從租
      non-lease components from lease component, and instead                 賃部分區分非租賃部分，而是作為單一租
      account for the lease component and any associated non-lease           賃部分入賬列為租賃部分及任何相關非租
      components as a single lease component.                                賃部分。

     ACCOUNTING POLICIES (Continued)

     Lease (Continued)                                                        租賃（續）

     The Group as a lessee (Continued)                                        本集團作為承租人（續）

     Short-term leases and leases of low-value assets                         短期租賃及低價值資產租賃

     The Group applies the short-term lease recognition exemption             對 於 租 期 自 開 始 日 期 起 計 為 12 個 月 或 以
     to leases of staff quarters, office and equipment that have              內且並無包含購買選擇權的員工宿舍、辦
     a lease term of 12 months or less from the commencement                  公室及設備租賃，本集團應用短期租賃確
     date and do not contain a purchase option. It also applies the           認豁免。本集團亦對低價值資產租賃應用
     recognition exemption for lease of low-value assets. Lease               確認豁免。短期租賃及低價值資產租賃的
     payments on short-term leases and leases of low-value assets             租賃付款按直線基準或另一系統化基準於
     are recognised as expense on a straight-line basis or another            租期內確認為開支。
     systematic basis over the lease term.

     Right-of-use assets                                                      使用權資產

     The cost of right-of-use asset includes:                                 使用權資產的成本包括：

     •      the amount of the initial measurement of the lease                •        租賃負債的初步計量金額；
            liability;

     •      any lease payments made at or before the                          •        於開始日期或之前作出的任何租賃
            commencement date, less any lease incentives received;                     付款，減任何已收租賃優惠；

     •      any initial direct costs incurred by the Group; and               •        本集團產生的任何初始直接成本；
                                                                                       及

     •      an estimate of costs to be incurred by the Group                  •        本集團於拆解及搬遷相關資產、復
            in dismantling and removing the underlying assets,                         原相關資產所在場地或復原相關資
            restoring the site on which it is located or restoring the                 產至租賃的條款及條件所規定的狀
            underlying asset to the condition required by the terms                    況而產生的成本估計。
            and conditions of the lease.

     Right-of-use assets are measured at cost, less any accumulated           使用權資產按成本減任何累計折舊及減值
     depreciation and impairment losses, and adjusted for any                 虧損計量，並就任何租賃負債重新計量作
     remeasurement of lease liabilities.                                      出調整。

     Right-of-use assets are depreciated on a straight-line basis over        使用權資產於其估計可使用年期及租期（以
     the shorter of its estimated useful life and the lease term.             較短者為準）內按直線法計提折舊。

     When a right-of-use asset meets the definition of investment             當使用權資產符合投資物業之定義時則計
     property, it is included in investment properties.                       入投資物業。

      and initially measured at fair value. Adjustments to fair value at        告準則第 9 號入賬，並初步按公平值計量。
      initial recognition are considered as additional lease payments           初始確認時對公平值的調整被視為額外租
      and included in the cost of right-of-use assets.                          賃付款並計入使用權資產成本。

      Lease liabilities                                                         租賃負債

      At the commencement date of a lease, the Group recognises                 於租賃開始日期，本集團按當日未付的租
      and measures the lease liability at the present value of                  賃付款現值確認及計量租賃負債。於計量
      lease payments that are unpaid at that date. In calculating               租賃付款現值時，倘租賃中隱含的利率不
      the present value of lease payments, the Group uses the                   易確定，則本集團於租賃開始日期使用增
      incremental borrowing rate at the lease commencement                      量借貸利率。
      date if the interest rate implicit in the lease is not readily
      determinable.

      The lease payments include:                                               租賃付款包括：

      •       fixed payments (including in-substance fixed payments)            •   定額付款（包括實質定額付款），扣
              less any lease incentives receivable;                                 減任何應收租賃獎勵；

      •       variable lease payments that depend on an index or a              •   基於某項指數或比率的可變租賃付
              rate, initially measured using the index or rate as at the            款，於開始日期初始使用指數或利
              commencement date;                                                    率計量；

      •       amounts expected to be payable by the Group under                 •   本集團預期根據剩餘價值擔保支付
              residual value guarantees;                                            的金額；

      •       the exercise price of a purchase option if the Group is           •   本集團合理確定行使的購買選擇權
              reasonably certain to exercise the option; and                        的行使價；及

      •       payments of penalties for terminating a lease, if the             •   倘租期反映本集團行使終止租賃選
              lease term reflects the Group exercising an option to                 擇權時，有關終止租賃的罰款。
              terminate the lease.

      After the commencement date, lease liabilities are adjusted by            於開始日期後，租賃負債通過利息增加及
      interest accretion and lease payments.                                    租賃付款進行調整。

      leases.

     Government grants                                                       政府補助

     Government grants are not recognised until there is reasonable          在合理保證本集團會遵守政府補助的附帶
     assurance that the Group will comply with the conditions                條件以及將會得到補助後，政府補助方予
     attaching to them and that the grants will be received.                 以確認。

     Government grants are recognised in profit or loss on                   政府補助於本集團確認有關補助擬抵銷的
     a systematic basis over the periods in which the Group                  相關成本為支出期間按系統化基準於損益
     recognises as expenses the related costs for which the grants           確認。與作為已招致的開支或虧損的賠償
     are intended to compensate. Government grants relate to                 或為向本集團提供直接財務幫助而應收取
     income that are receivable as compensation for expenses or              的與收入相關且無未來相關成本的政府補
     losses already incurred or for the purpose of giving immediate          助在相關補助可予收取期間於損益確認。
     financial support to the Group with no future related costs are         該等補助於「其他收入、收益及虧損」項下
     recognised in profit or loss in the period in which they become         呈列。
     receivable. Such grants are presented under “other income,
     gains and losses”.

     Retirement benefit costs                                                退休福利成本

     as and when employees rendered the services. All short-                 員福利確認為開支，除非另一項國際財務
     term employee benefits are recognised as an expense unless              報告準則要求或允許將有關福利納入資產
     another IFRS requires or permits the inclusion of the benefit in        成本，則作別論。
     the cost of an asset.

     (such as wages and salaries, annual leave and sick leave) after         得的福利（例如工資薪金、年假及病假）確
     deducting any amount already paid.                                      認負債。

     Taxation                                                                    稅項

     Income tax expense represents the sum of the tax currently                  所得稅開支指即期應付稅項與遞延稅項的
     payable and deferred tax.                                                   總和。

     The tax currently payable is based on taxable profit during                 即期應付稅項乃按年內應課稅溢利計算。
     the year. Taxable profit differs from profit before tax because             應課稅溢利由於在其他年度的應課稅或可
     of income or expense that are taxable or deductible in other                扣稅的收入或開支或從未課稅或扣稅的項
     years and items that are never taxable or deductible. The                   目而有別於除稅前溢利。本集團的即期稅
     Group’s liability for current tax is calculated using tax rates that        項負債乃按各報告期末前已實施或實質上
     have been enacted or substantively enacted at the end of each               已實施的稅率計算。
     reporting period.

     Deferred tax is recognised on temporary differences                         遞延稅項按綜合財務報表中資產及負債的
     between the carrying amounts of assets and liabilities in the               賬面值與計算應課稅溢利時使用的相應稅
     consolidated financial statements and the corresponding tax                 基的暫時差額確認。遞延稅項負債通常會
     bases used in the computation of taxable profit. Deferred tax               就所有應課稅暫時差額確認。遞延稅項資
     liabilities are generally recognised for all taxable temporary              產一般僅在可能有應課稅溢利供可扣稅暫
     differences. Deferred tax assets are generally recognised for               時差額抵銷時，方會就所有可扣稅暫時差
     all deductible temporary difference to the extent that it is                額確認。倘暫時差額源自商譽或對應課稅
     probable that taxable profits will be available against which               溢利及會計溢利均不造成影響的交易中初
     those deductible temporary differences can be utilised. Such                步 確 認（除 業 務 合 併 外）的 其 他 資 產 及 負
     assets and liabilities are not recognised if the temporary                  債，則不會確認該等資產及負債。此外，
     difference arises from goodwill or from the initial recognition             倘暫時差額源自初步確認的商譽，則不會
     (other than in a business combination) of other assets and                  確認遞延稅項負債。
     liabilities in a transaction that affects neither the taxable profit
     nor the accounting profit. In addition, deferred tax liabilities
     are not recognised if the temporary difference arises from the
     initial recognition of goodwill.

     Deferred tax liabilities are recognised for taxable temporary               遞延稅項負債乃就與附屬公司、聯營公司
     differences associated with interest in subsidiaries, associates            及合營企業的權益相關的應課稅暫時差額
     and joint venture except where the Group is able to control                 予以確認，惟本集團能控制暫時差額的撥
     the reversal of the temporary difference and it is probable that            回及暫時差額可能於可見將來不會撥回則
     the temporary difference will not reverse in the foreseeable                作別論。與該等投資相關的可扣稅暫時差
     future. Deferred tax assets arising from deductible temporary               額所產生的遞延稅項資產，僅於可能有足
     differences associated with such investments are only                       夠應課稅溢利可以使用暫時差額的利益且
     recognised to the extent that it is probable that there will be             預期於可見將來可以撥回時予以確認。
     sufficient taxable profits against which to utilise the benefits of
     the temporary differences and they are expected to reverse in
     the foreseeable future.

      The carrying amount of deferred tax assets is reviewed at the            遞延稅項資產的賬面值於各報告期末作出
      end of each reporting period and reduced to the extent that              檢討，並於不再有足夠應課稅溢利令致全
      it is no longer probable that sufficient taxable profits will be         部或部分資產可以收回時作出相應調減。
      available to allow all or part of the asset to be recovered.

      Deferred tax assets and liabilities are measured at the tax rates        遞延稅項資產及負債乃按預期於償還負債
      that are expected to apply in the period in which the liability          或變現資產期間適用的稅率（以報告期末
      is settled or the asset is realised, based on tax rates (and tax         已 實 施 或 實 質 上 已 實 施 的 稅 率（及 稅 法）
      laws) that have been enacted or substantively enacted by the             為基準）計量。
      end of the reporting period.

      The measurement of deferred tax liabilities and assets reflects          遞延稅項負債及資產的計量反映本集團於
      the tax consequences that would follow from the manner in                各報告期末預期收回或償還其資產及負債
      which the Group expects, at the end of each reporting period,            的賬面值的方式將產生的稅務後果。
      to recover or settle the carrying amount of its assets and
      liabilities.

      For the purposes of measuring deferred tax for investment                為計量使用公平值模型計量的投資物業遞
      properties that are measured using the fair value model, the             延稅項，除非推翻有關假設，否則假設有
      carrying amounts of such properties are presumed to be                   關物業的賬面值可透過銷售全數收回。當
      recovered entirely through sale, unless the presumption is               投資物業可折舊及於目的為隨時間推移而
      rebutted. The presumption is rebutted when the investment                非通過銷售消耗投資物業的絕大部分經濟
      property is depreciable and is held within a business model              利益的商業模式下持有，則推翻假設。
      whose objective is to consume substantially all of the
      economic benefits embodied in the investment property over
      time, rather than through sale.

      For the purposes of measuring deferred tax for leasing                   為本集團確認使用權資產及相關租賃負債
      transactions in which the Group recognises the right-of-                 的租賃交易計量遞延稅項時，本集團首先
      use assets and the related lease liabilities, the Group first            釐定稅項減免是否源自使用權資產或租賃
      determines whether the tax deductions are attributable to the            負債。

     revaluation less subsequent accumulated amortisation and                 日期的公平值減其後累計攤銷及任何累計
     any accumulated impairment losses, on the same basis as                  減值虧損）呈報。
     intangible assets that are acquired separately.

     An intangible asset is derecognised on disposal, or when no              無形資產於出售或預期使用或出售無形資
     future economic benefits are expected from use or disposal.              產不會產生未來經濟利益時終止確認。終
     Gains and losses arising from derecognition of an intangible             止確認無形資產產生的收益及虧損乃按出
     asset, measured as the difference between the net disposal               售所得款項淨額與該資產賬面值之間的差
     proceeds and the carrying amount of the asset, are recognised            額計算，並於終止確認該資產時在損益內
     in profit or loss when the asset is derecognised.                        確認。

      The effective interest method is a method of calculating the            實際利率法是計算金融資產或金融負債的
      amortised cost of a financial asset or financial liability and          攤銷成本及於相關期間分配利息收入及利
      of allocating interest income and interest expense over the             息開支的方法。實際利率為於金融資產或
      relevant period. The effective interest rate is the rate that           金 融 負 債 的 預 計 年 期 或（如 適 用）較 短 期
      exactly discounts estimated future cash receipts and payments           間內可將估計未來現金收入及付款（包括
      (including all fees paid or received that form an integral part         構成實際利率不可或缺部分的所有已付或
      of the effective interest rate, transaction costs and other             已收費用、交易成本及其他溢價或折讓）
      premiums or discounts) through the expected life of the                 準確貼現至初步確認的賬面淨值的比率。
      financial asset or financial liability, or, where appropriate,
      a shorter period, to the net carrying amount on initial
      recognition.

             and

             specified dates to cash flows that are solely payments                生之現金流量僅為支付本金及未償

     The Group performs impairment assessment under ECL model                 本集團根據預期信貸虧損模式對金融資產

     possible default events over the expected life of the relevant          內發生所有可能的違約事件而導致的預期

     of lifetime ECL that is expected to result from default events          指 預 期 於 報 告 日 期 後 12 個 月 內 可 能 發 生

     Assessments are done based on the Group’s historical credit             損。預期信貸虧損根據本集團過往信貸虧

     debtors, general economic conditions and an assessment of               素、一般經濟狀況及於報告日期對當前狀
     both the current conditions at the reporting date as well as            況及未來狀況預測的評估而作出調整。本
     the forecast of future conditions. The Group always recognises          集團一直就貿易應收款項確認全期預期信
     lifetime ECL for trade-related receivables.                             貸虧損。

     For all other instruments, the Group measures the loss                  就 所 有 其 他 工 具 而 言，本 集 團 計 量 與 12
     allowance equal to 12-month ECL, unless when there                      個月預期信貸虧損等額的虧損撥備，除非
     has been a significant increase in credit risk since initial            信貸風險自初始確認以來已大幅增加，則
     recognition, in which case the Group recognises lifetime ECL.           本集團確認全期預期信貸虧損。評估是否
     The assessment of whether lifetime ECL should be recognised             應確認全期預期信貸虧損，乃基於自初始

      (ii)    Definition of default                                               (ii)    違約的定義

              For internal credit risk management, the Group                               就內部信貸風險管理而言，本集團
              considers an event of default occurs when information                        認為內部發現或從外部資源取得的
              developed internally or obtained from external sources                       資料顯示債務人不可能向其債權人
              indicates that the debtor is unlikely to pay its creditors,                 （包括本集團）悉數付款（不計及本
              including the Group, in full (without taking into                            集 團 持 有 的 任 何 抵 押 品）時，即 屬
              account any collaterals held by the Group).                                  發生違約事件。

              Irrespective of the above, the Group considers that                         不論上文所述者，本集團認為，倘
              default has occurred when a financial asset is more                         金 融 資 產 逾 期 超 過 90 天 時 則 發 生
              than 90 days past due unless the Group has reasonable                       違約，除非本集團有合理及有理據
              and supportable information to demonstrate that a                           的資料證明更寬鬆的違約標準屬更
              more lagging default criterion is more appropriate.                         合適，則另作別論。

      (iii)   Credit-impaired financial assets                                    (iii)   信貸減值的金融資產

              events that have a detrimental impact on the estimated                      流量產生不利影響的一起或多起違
              future cash flows of that financial asset have occurred.                    約事件之時，該金融資產即出現信
              Evidence that a financial asset is credit-impaired                          貸減值。金融資產出現信貸減值的
              includes observable data about the following events:                        證據包括與下列事件相關的可觀察
                                                                                          數據：

              •      significant financial difficulty of the issuer or the                •    發行人或借款人陷入嚴重財
                     borrower;                                                                 困；

              •      a breach of contract, such as a default or past                      •    違反合約，如違約或逾期事
                     due event;                                                                件；

              •      the lender(s) of the borrower, for economic or                       •    借款人的貸款人出於與借款
                     contractual reasons relating to the borrower’s                            人財困相關的經濟或合約原
                     financial difficulty, having granted to the                               因，而向借款人授予貸款人
                     borrower a concession(s) that the lender(s)                               原本不會考慮的優惠；或
                     would not otherwise consider; or

     (iv)   Write-off policy                                                  (iv)   撇銷政策

            The Group writes off a financial asset when there is                     本集團在有資料顯示交易對手陷入
            information indicating that the counterparty is in severe                嚴重財務困難，且無實際收回資產
            financial difficulty and there is no realistic prospect of               可能之時（例如交易對手遭受清盤
            recovery, for example, when the counterparty has been                    或已進入破產程序（以較早發生著
            placed under liquidation or has entered into bankruptcy                  為準））撇銷金融資產。在考慮法律
            proceedings, whichever occurs sooner. Financial assets                   意見（如適當）後，已撇銷金融資產
            written off may still be subject to enforcement activities               仍可根據本集團之收回程序實施強
            under the Group’s recovery procedures, taking into                       制執行活動。撇銷構成終止確認事
            account legal advice where appropriate. A write-off                      件。任何其後收回資產於損益中確
            constitutes a derecognition event. Any subsequent                        認。
            recoveries are recognised in profit or loss.

     (v)    Measurement and recognition of ECL                                (v)    預期信貸虧損之計量及確認

            The measurement of ECL is a function of the                              預期信貸虧損之計量為違約概率、
            probability of default, loss given default (i.e. the                     違約損失率（即出現違約時的損失
            magnitude of the loss if there is a default) and the                     程 度）及 違 約 風 險 的 函 數。違 約 可
            exposure at default. The assessment of the probability                   能性及違約損失率之評估乃根據過
            of default and loss given default is based on historical                 往數據及前瞻性資料進行。預期信
            data and forward-looking information. Estimation of                      貸虧損的估計反映無偏頗及概率加
            ECL reflects an unbiased and probability-weighted                        權數額，其乃根據加權的相應違約
            amount that is determined with the respective risks                      風險而釐定。本集團經考慮過往信
            of default occurring as the weights. The Group uses                      貸虧損經驗後使用撥備矩陣並採用
            a practical expedient in estimating ECL on certain                       實際權益法估計若干貿易應收款項
            trade receivables using a provision matrix taking into                   的預期信貸虧損，並按毋需花費不
            consideration historical credit loss experience, adjusted                必要成本或精力可取得的前瞻性資
            for forward looking information that is available                        料調整。
            without undue cost or effort.

            Generally, the ECL is estimated as the difference                        一般而言，預期信貸虧損按根據合
            between all contractual cash flows that are due to the                   約應付予本集團之所有合約現金流
            Group in accordance with the contract and all the cash                   量與本集團預期收取之所有現金流
            flows that the Group expects to receive, discounted                      量之間的差額估計，並按首次確認
            at the effective interest rate determined at initial                     時釐定之實際利率貼現。
            recognition.

     Derecognition of financial assets                                         取消確認金融資產

     The Group derecognises a financial asset only when the                    僅於資產現金流量之合約權利屆滿時，或
     contractual rights to the cash flows from the asset expire, or            將金融資產所有權之絕大部分風險及回報
     when it transfers the financial asset and substantially all the           轉讓予另一實體時，本集團方會取消確認
     risks and rewards of ownership of the asset to another entity.            金融資產。倘本集團保留已轉讓金融資產
     If the Group retains substantially all the risks and rewards              所有權之絕大部分風險及回報，則本集團
     of ownership of a transferred financial asset, the Group                  繼續確認該金融資產且亦就已收取所得款
     continues to recognise the financial asset and also recognises            項確認抵押借款。
     a collateralised borrowing for the proceeds received.

     On derecognition of a financial asset measured at amortised               於取消確認一項按攤銷成本計量的金融資
     cost, the difference between the asset’s carrying amount                  產時，該項資產賬面值與已收及應收代價
     and the sum of the consideration received and receivable is               總和之間的差額會於損益中確認。
     recognised in profit or loss.

     Financial liabilities and equity                                          金融負債及權益

     Classification as debt and equity                                         分類為債務及權益

     Debt and equity instruments are classified as either financial            債務及股本工具乃根據所訂立合約安排及
     liabilities or as equity in accordance with the contractual               金融負債與股本工具之釋義分類為金融負
     arrangements and the definitions of a financial liability and an          債或股本。
     equity instrument.

     Equity instrument                                                         股本工具

     An equity instrument is any contract that evidences a residual            股本工具乃證明本集團資產經扣除其所有
     interest in the assets of the Group after deducting all of its            負債後的剩餘權益之任何合約。集團實體
     liabilities. Equity instruments issued by the group entities are          發行之股本工具以所收所得款項減直接發
     recorded at the proceeds received, net of direct issue costs.             行成本確認。

     Financial liabilities                                                     金融負債

     All financial liabilities are subsequently measured at amortised          所有金融負債其後使用實際利息法或按公
     cost using the effective interest method or at FVTPL.                     平值計入其他全面收益計量。

      Contingent liabilities                                                     或然負債

      A contingent liability is a present obligation arising from past           或然負債為由過去事項產生的現時義務，
      events but is not recognised because it is not probable that               但由於不太可能需要經濟利益的流出清償
      an outflow of resources embodying economic benefits will                   該義務，或者該義務的金額無法可靠計量，
      be required to settle the obligation or the amount of the                  因此未予確認。
      obligation cannot be measured with sufficient reliability.

      Where the Group is jointly and severally liable for an                     倘本集團對某項義務負有連帶責任，則該
      obligation, the part of the obligation that is expected to be              義務中預期由其他方履行的部分被視為或
      met by other parties is treated as a contingent liability and it is        然負債，不於綜合財務報表確認。
      not recognised in the consolidated financial statements.

      The Group assesses continually to determine whether an                     本集團持續進行評估，以確定經濟利益是
      outflow of resources embodying economic benefits has                       否可能流出。倘以前作為或然負債處理的
      become probable. If it becomes probable that an outflow                    項目很可能需要有未來經濟利益的流出，
      of future economic benefits will be required for an item                   則在可能性發生變化的報告期內於綜合財
      previously dealt with as a contingent liability, a provision is            務報表中確認撥備，除非在極少數情況下
      recognised in the consolidated financial statements in the                 無法做出可靠估計。
      reporting period in which the change in probability occurs,
      except in the extremely rare circumstances where no reliable
      estimate can be made.

      Events after the reporting period                                          報告期後事項

      Events after the reporting period that provide additional                  提供有關本集團於報告期末狀況或顯示持
      information about the Group’s position at the end of the                   續經營假設並不適當的額外資料的報告期
      reporting period or those that indicate the going concern                  後事項均屬於調整事項，並於綜合財務報
      assumption is not appropriate are adjusting events and are                 表內反映。並非屬調整事項的報告期後事
      reflected in the consolidated financial statements. Events after           項如屬重大，則於綜合財務報表附註中披
      the reporting period that are not adjusting events are disclosed           露。
      in the notes to the consolidated financial statements when
      material.

     facts and circumstances which results in a downward revision               金流量及貼現率受金融市場中較高程度估
     of future cash flows, a material impairment loss may arise.                計及波動影響。

      instruments

      When the fair values of financial assets recorded in the               倘於財務狀況表列賬的金融資產公平值無
      statement of financial position cannot be derived from active          法按活躍市場報價計量，其公平值乃利用
      markets, their fair values are determined using valuation              估值方法（包括使用近期可比較公平交易
      techniques including the use of comparable recent arm’s                及其他市場參與者常用的其他估值方法）
      length transactions and other valuation techniques commonly            計量。該等模型的輸入值乃盡可能從可觀
      used by other market participants. The inputs to these models          察市場取得，但倘無法從可觀察市場取得，
      are taken from observable markets where possible, but where            則於釐定公平值時須作出一定程度的判斷。
      this is not feasible, a degree of judgement is required in             判斷包括考慮輸入值，如權益價值、波幅
      establishing fair values. The judgements include considerations        及貼現率。有關該等因素的假設變動可能
      of inputs such as the implied equity value, volatility and             影響金融工具呈報的公平值。本集團將該
      discount rate. Changes in assumptions about these factors              等投資的公平值分類為第三級。更多詳情
      could affect the reported fair value of financial instruments.         載於綜合財務報表附註 42。
      The Group classifies the fair value of these investments
      as Level 3. Further details are included in Note 42 to the
      consolidated financial statements.

      tax rate of 15% for the three years from 1 January 2023 to               日至二零二五年十二月三十一日及於二
      31 December 2025 and from 1 January 2024 to 31 December                  零二四年一月一日至二零二六年十二月

      The above items of property, plant and equipment are                       上述物業、廠房及設備項目乃經計及估計
      depreciated over their useful lives as follows, after taking into          剩餘價值後於下列可使用年期內按直線法
      account the estimated residual value, on a straight-line basis:            進行折舊：

      Buildings                       20 years                                   樓宇                             20 年
      Office equipment                3 to 5 years                               辦公室設備                       3至5年
      Motor vehicles                  3 to 5 years                               汽車                          3至5年

24.   TRADE AND OTHER RECEIVABLES (Continued)                                24.   貿易及其他應收款項（續）

      The Group writes off a trade receivable when there is                        倘有資料顯示交易對手面對嚴重財務困難
      information indicating that the counterparty is in severe                    且收回款項的機率不大（如交易對手遭清
      financial difficulty and there is no realistic prospect of recovery,         盤或啟動破產程序），本集團將撇銷貿易
      e.g. when the counterparty has been placed under liquidation                 應收款項。在考慮法律意見（如適當）後，
      or has entered into bankruptcy proceedings. Trade receivables                已撇銷的貿易應收款項仍可根據本集團的
      written off may still be subject to enforcement activities under             追討程序實施強制執行。任何收回款項均
      the Group’s recovery procedures, taking into account legal                   於損益中確認。
      advice where appropriate. Any recoveries made are recognised
      in profit or loss.

      Included in the allowances of loss for trade receivables are                 貿易應收款項虧損撥備分別包括結餘總額
      collectively assessed trade receivables with an aggregate                    為人民幣 364,925,000 元（二零二四年：人
      balance of RMB364,925,000 (2024: RMB293,265,000) and                         民幣 293,265,000 元）的共同評估貿易應收
      individually assessed trade receivables with an aggregate                    款 項 及 結 餘 總 額 為 人 民 幣 119,117,000 元
      balance of RMB119,117,000 (2024: RMB100,929,000),                            （二 零 二 四 年：人 民 幣 100,929,000 元）的
      respectively, details of which are set out in Note 42.                       個別評估貿易應收款項，其詳情載於附註

34.   RETIREMENT BENEFIT PLANS                                            34.   退休福利計劃

      According to the relevant laws and regulations in the PRC,                根據中國相關法律及法規，本公司的中國
      the Company’s PRC subsidiaries are required to participate                附屬公司須參與由地方市政府管理的定額
      in a defined contribution retirement scheme administrated                 供款退休計劃。該等中國附屬公司乃以平
      by the local municipal government. These PRC subsidiaries                 均僱員薪金按地方市政府同意的既定百分
      contribute funds which are calculated on certain percentage               比計算之金額向計劃支付供款，為僱員的
      of the average employee salary as agreed by local municipal               退休福利提供資金。本集團對退休福利計
      government to the scheme to fund the retirement benefits                  劃應負的主要責任為根據計劃作出所須供
      of the employees. The principal obligation of the Group                   款。對該計劃的供款即時歸屬，本集團並
      with respect to the retirement benefits scheme is to make                 無可用於降低現有供款水平的已沒收供款。
      the required contributions under the scheme. Contributions
      to the scheme vest immediately and there is no forfeited
      contributions that may be used by the Group to reduce the
      existing level of contributions.

      The Group also operates a Mandatory Provident Fund Scheme                 本集團亦為香港所有合資格僱員設立強制
      for all qualified employees in Hong Kong. The assets of the               性公積金計劃。計劃之資產與本集團資產
      scheme are held separately from those of the Group, in funds              分開持有，存置於受託人管理之基金。本
      under the control of trustee. The Group contributes 5% of                 集團按相關薪金成本之 5% 向該計劃供款，
      relevant payroll costs to the scheme and the same amount                  而僱員按相同金額供款。對該計劃的供款
      is matched by employees. Contributions to the scheme vest                 即時歸屬，本集團並無可用於降低現有供
      immediately and there is no forfeited contributions that                  款水平的已沒收供款。
      may be used by the Group to reduce the existing level of
      contributions.

      The Group recognised the retirement benefit contributions of              截至二零二五年十二月三十一日止年
      RMB27,598,000 (2024: RMB29,465,000) for the year ended                    度，本 集 團 確 認 退 休 福 利 供 款 人 民 幣

41.   CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                         41.   資本風險管理

      The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities in              本集團管理其資本以確保本集團旗下實體
      the Group will be able to continue as a going concern                 將可持續經營，同時通過優化債務及權益
      while maximising the return to shareholders through the               結餘為股東帶來最大回報。本集團整體策
      optimisation of the debt and equity balance. The Group’s              略與去年維持不變。
      overall strategy remains unchanged from the prior year.

      The capital structure of the Group consists of borrowings,            本集團的資本架構包括借款（扣除現金及
      net of cash and cash equivalents, and equity attributable to          現金等價物）及本公司擁有人應佔股本，
      owners of the Company, comprising share capital, reserves             包括股本、儲備及保留溢利
      and retained profits.

      The management of the Group reviews the capital structure             本集團管理層定期檢討資本架構及考慮資
      periodically and considers the cost of capital and the risks          本成本及與各類別資本有關的風險，並通
      associated with each class of capital and balance its overall         過派付股息及發行新股以及發行新債務或
      capital structure through the payment of dividends and                贖回現有債務平衡其整體資本架構。
      new share issues as well as the issue of new debts or the
      redemption of existing debts.
